            if (q.repeat != Player.REPEAT_MODE_OFF) player.repeatMode = q.repeat
            if (q.shuffle) player.shuffleModeEnabled = true
            return
        }
        c.queue.source.value = QueueSource(QueueKind.OTHER, q.title)
        val (index, position) = PlaceKeeper.start(q.songs.map { it.id }, q.index, q.positionMs, keptPlace(), placeIsNewer = true)
        player.setMediaItems(q.songs.map { it.toMediaItem() }, index.coerceIn(0, q.songs.lastIndex), position)
        // Shuffle and repeat as they were, in the same order.
        if (q.shuffle) {
            if (q.shuffleOrder.size == q.songs.size && q.shuffleOrder.sorted() == q.songs.indices.toList()) {
                player.setShuffleOrder(HistoryShuffleOrder(q.shuffleOrder.toIntArray()) { player.currentMediaItemIndex })
            } else reshuffle()
            player.shuffleModeEnabled = true
        }
        if (q.repeat != Player.REPEAT_MODE_OFF) player.repeatMode = q.repeat
        // The mini player shows this song: get it ready, so Play starts it straight away.
        player.currentMediaItem?.mediaId?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }?.let { id -> scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) { c.warmUp(id) } }
        // And the one after it (otherwise only a song coming up warms its successor).
        prefetchNext()
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ Session

    private inner class LibraryCallback : MediaLibrarySession.Callback {
        override fun onConnect(session: MediaSession, controller: MediaSession.ControllerInfo): MediaSession.ConnectionResult {
            if (isCar(session, controller)) {
                carControllers += controller
                carLyrics.carConnected = true
            }
            if (controller.packageName != packageName) {
                AudioDiagnostics.log("control", "connected: ${controller.packageName} (${if (controller.isTrusted) "trusted" else "not trusted"})")
            }
            return MediaSession.ConnectionResult.AcceptedResultBuilder(session, controller)
                .setAvailableSessionCommands(
                    MediaSession.ConnectionResult.DEFAULT_SESSION_AND_LIBRARY_COMMANDS.buildUpon().apply { customCommands.forEach { add(it) } }.build()
                )
                // Every app may play, pause and skip (#178, #187). Media3 1.11 gives apps it doesn't count as trusted only
                // the read-only player commands, so KDE Connect, "dynamic island" apps and launcher widgets showed the song
                // but their pause and skip were dropped without a word. Android already limits who can reach a media
                // session (the system, notification listeners, apps bound to the exported service), and a music player is
                // there to be controlled.
                .setAvailablePlayerCommands(MediaSession.ConnectionResult.DEFAULT_PLAYER_COMMANDS)
                .build()
        }

        override fun onDisconnected(session: MediaSession, controller: MediaSession.ControllerInfo) {
            if (carControllers.remove(controller)) carLyrics.carConnected = carControllers.isNotEmpty()
        }

        // Every play, pause or skip another app sends (a widget, KDE Connect, a watchâ€¦), so a diagnostics log shows
        // whether a command arrived at all and from whom (#187, #178). Prism's own screens aren't logged.
        override fun onPlayerCommandRequest(session: MediaSession, controller: MediaSession.ControllerInfo, playerCommand: Int): Int {
            if (AudioDiagnostics.enabled && controller.packageName != packageName) {
                val what = when (playerCommand) {
                    Player.COMMAND_PLAY_PAUSE -> "play/pause"
                    Player.COMMAND_STOP -> "stop"
                    Player.COMMAND_PREPARE -> "prepare"
                    Player.COMMAND_SEEK_TO_NEXT, Player.COMMAND_SEEK_TO_NEXT_MEDIA_ITEM -> "next"
                    Player.COMMAND_SEEK_TO_PREVIOUS, Player.COMMAND_SEEK_TO_PREVIOUS_MEDIA_ITEM -> "previous"
                    Player.COMMAND_SEEK_IN_CURRENT_MEDIA_ITEM -> "seek"
                    else -> null
                }
                if (what != null) {
                    AudioDiagnostics.log(
                        "control",
                        "$what from ${controller.packageName} (${if (controller.isTrusted) "trusted" else "not trusted"}, " +
                            "service ${if (inForeground()) "in the foreground" else "in the background"}, ${this@PlaybackService.javaClass.simpleName})",
                    )
                }
            }
            return super.onPlayerCommandRequest(session, controller, playerCommand)
        }

        override fun onMediaButtonEvent(session: MediaSession, controllerInfo: MediaSession.ControllerInfo, intent: Intent): Boolean {
            val key = runCatching {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                intent.getParcelableExtra<android.view.KeyEvent>(Intent.EXTRA_KEY_EVENT)
            }.getOrNull()
            if (key?.action == android.view.KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) {
                AudioDiagnostics.log("control", "media button ${android.view.KeyEvent.keyCodeToString(key.keyCode)} from ${controllerInfo.packageName}")
            }
            // The notification swiped away (Media3 sends it as a media button with this extra): the listener is closing
            // Prism (Reddit, 1.7.7: it came back once, then sat in "background activity" until stopped by hand).
            if (intent.getBooleanExtra(NOTIFICATION_DISMISSED, false)) {
                val handled = super.onMediaButtonEvent(session, controllerInfo, intent)
                scope.launch { closedFromNotification() }
                return handled
            }
            return super.onMediaButtonEvent(session, controllerInfo, intent)
        }

        override fun onCustomCommand(
            session: MediaSession,
            controller: MediaSession.ControllerInfo,
            customCommand: SessionCommand,
            args: Bundle,
        ): ListenableFuture<SessionResult> {
            when (customCommand.customAction) {
                likeCommand.customAction -> player.currentMediaItem?.toSong()?.let { c.library.toggleLike(it) }
                shuffleCommand.customAction -> {
                    if (!player.shuffleModeEnabled) reshuffle()
                    player.shuffleModeEnabled = !player.shuffleModeEnabled
                }
                repeatCommand.customAction -> player.repeatMode = when (player.repeatMode) {
                    Player.REPEAT_MODE_OFF -> Player.REPEAT_MODE_ALL
                    Player.REPEAT_MODE_ALL -> Player.REPEAT_MODE_ONE
                    else -> Player.REPEAT_MODE_OFF
                }
                lyricsCommand.customAction -> carLyrics.toggle()
                radioCommand.customAction -> startRadio()
                dislikeCommand.customAction -> player.currentMediaItem?.toSong()?.let { song ->
                    c.library.setDisliked(song, true)
                    if (player.hasNextMediaItem()) player.seekToNextMediaItem()
                }
                back10Command.customAction -> player.seekTo((player.currentPosition - 10_000).coerceAtLeast(0))
                ahead30Command.customAction -> {
                    val end = player.duration.takeIf { it != C.TIME_UNSET } ?: Long.MAX_VALUE
                    player.seekTo((player.currentPosition + 30_000).coerceAtMost((end - 1_000).coerceAtLeast(0)))
                }
            }
            return Futures.immediateFuture(SessionResult(SessionResult.RESULT_SUCCESS))
        }

        // ---- Browsing (Android Auto, Assistant, other media browsers)

        override fun onGetLibraryRoot(
            session: MediaLibrarySession,
            browser: MediaSession.ControllerInfo,
            params: LibraryParams?,
        ): ListenableFuture<LibraryResult<MediaItem>> =
            // Android Auto / the system ask for a "recent" root to offer a resume card. The root's hints say how the car shows
            // folders (cover tiles with the grid on) unless a folder says otherwise.
            Futures.immediateFuture(LibraryResult.ofItem(
                if (params?.isRecent == true) tree.recentRoot() else tree.root(),
                if (params?.isRecent == true) params else tree.rootExtras()?.let { hints ->
                    LibraryParams.Builder().setExtras(android.os.Bundle(params?.extras ?: android.os.Bundle.EMPTY).apply { putAll(hints) })
                        .setOffline(params?.isOffline == true).setSuggested(params?.isSuggested == true).build()
                } ?: params,
            ))

        override fun onGetChildren(
            session: MediaLibrarySession,
            browser: MediaSession.ControllerInfo,
            parentId: String,
            page: Int,
            pageSize: Int,
            params: LibraryParams?,
        ): ListenableFuture<LibraryResult<ImmutableList<MediaItem>>> = scope.future {
            val all = tree.children(parentId)
            val from = (page * pageSize).coerceAtMost(all.size)
            LibraryResult.ofItemList(all.subList(from, (from + pageSize).coerceAtMost(all.size)), params)
        }

        override fun onGetItem(
            session: MediaLibrarySession,
            browser: MediaSession.ControllerInfo,
            mediaId: String,
        ): ListenableFuture<LibraryResult<MediaItem>> = scope.future {
            tree.item(mediaId)?.let { LibraryResult.ofItem(it, null) } ?: LibraryResult.ofError(SessionError.ERROR_BAD_VALUE)
        }

        override fun onSearch(
            session: MediaLibrarySession,
            browser: MediaSession.ControllerInfo,
            query: String,
            params: LibraryParams?,
        ): ListenableFuture<LibraryResult<Void>> = scope.future {
            val count = tree.search(query)
            session.notifySearchResultChanged(browser, query, count, params)
            LibraryResult.ofVoid()
        }

        override fun onGetSearchResult(
            session: MediaLibrarySession,
            browser: MediaSession.ControllerInfo,
            query: String,
            page: Int,
            pageSize: Int,
            params: LibraryParams?,
        ): ListenableFuture<LibraryResult<ImmutableList<MediaItem>>> = scope.future {
            // This query's results (searched now if the car asks for them without a search first), not the last search's.
            val all = tree.searchItems(query)
            // A car that asks for no pages gets page 0 of Int.MAX_VALUE: worked out in Long, so it can't overflow.
            val from = (page.toLong() * pageSize).coerceIn(0, all.size.toLong()).toInt()
            LibraryResult.ofItemList(all.subList(from, (from.toLong() + pageSize).coerceAtMost(all.size.toLong()).toInt()), params)
        }

        // ---- Playing what a controller picked

        override fun onAddMediaItems(
            mediaSession: MediaSession,
            controller: MediaSession.ControllerInfo,
            mediaItems: List<MediaItem>,
        ): ListenableFuture<List<MediaItem>> = scope.future {
            mediaItems.flatMap { item ->
                if (item.mediaId.startsWith(MediaTree.SHUFFLE)) tree.shuffleSongs(item.mediaId)?.second.orEmpty().shuffled().map { it.toMediaItem() }
                else if (item.mediaId.startsWith(MediaTree.DJ)) tree.shuffleSongs(item.mediaId)?.second.orEmpty().map { it.toMediaItem() }
                else listOfNotNull((item.toSong() ?: tree.song(item.mediaId, item))?.toMediaItem())
            }
        }

        override fun onSetMediaItems(
            mediaSession: MediaSession,
            controller: MediaSession.ControllerInfo,
            mediaItems: List<MediaItem>,
            startIndex: Int,
            startPositionMs: Long,
        ): ListenableFuture<MediaSession.MediaItemsWithStartPosition> = scope.future {
            val single = mediaItems.singleOrNull()
            // "Hey Google, play â€¦ on Prism" (the phone's VoicePlayActivity, or the car's voice button through the
            // session's play-from-search): words and voice extras instead of an id.
            if (single != null && single.mediaId.isBlank() && single.toSong() == null) {
                return@future playVoice(mediaSession, controller, single.requestMetadata.searchQuery, single.requestMetadata.extras)
                    .also { voiceAnsweredAt = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() }
            }
            // "Shuffle play" at the top of a list.
            if (single != null && single.mediaId.startsWith(MediaTree.SHUFFLE)) {
                tree.shuffleSongs(single.mediaId)?.takeIf { it.second.isNotEmpty() }?.let { (title, list) ->
                    c.queue.source.value = QueueSource(QueueKind.PLAYLIST, title)
                    c.queue.autoplayStart.value = -1
                    // A new queue, shuffled: DJ mode ends with the old one (Shuffle in DJ mode would deal a DJ set, #374).
                    c.dj.setOn(false)
                    player.shuffleModeEnabled = true
                    // Shuffled here too, so each "Shuffle play" is a new order rather than the player's last one.
                    return@future MediaSession.MediaItemsWithStartPosition(list.shuffled().map { it.toMediaItem() }, 0, C.TIME_UNSET)
                }
            }
            // "Play as a DJ set" at the top of a list: from the first song, then in the order that blends best (DJ mode,
            // switched on once the songs are in, as the phone's "Play as a DJ set" does).
            if (single != null && single.mediaId.startsWith(MediaTree.DJ)) {
                tree.shuffleSongs(single.mediaId)?.takeIf { it.second.size > 1 }?.let { (title, list) ->
                    c.queue.source.value = QueueSource(QueueKind.PLAYLIST, title)
                    c.queue.autoplayStart.value = -1
                    c.dj.setOn(false)
                    player.shuffleModeEnabled = false
                    val first = list.first().id
                    scope.launch {
                        kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeoutOrNull(5_000) {
                            while (player.currentMediaItem?.mediaId != first) kotlinx.coroutines.delay(100)
                        } ?: return@launch
                        c.dj.setOn(true)
                    }
                    return@future MediaSession.MediaItemsWithStartPosition(list.map { it.toMediaItem() }, 0, C.TIME_UNSET)
                }
            }
            // One song tapped in a list: play the list from that song.
            if (single != null && single.toSong() == null) {
                tree.folderOf(single.mediaId)?.let { (title, list) ->
                    val index = list.indexOfFirst { it.id == single.mediaId }.coerceAtLeast(0)
                    c.queue.source.value = QueueSource(QueueKind.PLAYLIST, title)
                    c.queue.autoplayStart.value = -1
                    return@future MediaSession.MediaItemsWithStartPosition(list.map { it.toMediaItem() }, index, startPositionMs)
                }
                tree.song(single.mediaId, single)?.let { song ->
                    c.queue.source.value = QueueSource(QueueKind.SINGLE, tr(R.string.autoplay), seedId = song.id)
                    c.queue.autoplayStart.value = -1
                    c.queue.autoplayContinuation = null
                    return@future MediaSession.MediaItemsWithStartPosition(listOf(song.toMediaItem()), 0, startPositionMs)
                }
            }
            val resolved = mediaItems.mapNotNull { item -> (item.toSong() ?: tree.song(item.mediaId, item))?.toMediaItem() }
            MediaSession.MediaItemsWithStartPosition(resolved, startIndex.coerceIn(0, (resolved.size - 1).coerceAtLeast(0)), startPositionMs)
        }

        /**
         * A spoken request ([VoiceSearch]): an artist's songs, an album, a playlist (the listener's own first), liked songs,
         * downloads, a song and its radio, or Search's Top result; "play music" carries on with the last queue. With
         * nothing found the queue is left as it was (it used to be cleared, so the car went silent) and the asker is told
         * why, which Android Auto shows.
         */
        private suspend fun playVoice(
            session: MediaSession,
            controller: MediaSession.ControllerInfo,
            query: String?,
            extras: Bundle?,
        ): MediaSession.MediaItemsWithStartPosition {
            val request = VoiceSearch.parse(query, extras)
            AudioDiagnostics.log("voice", "\"${query.orEmpty()}\" (${extras?.getString(android.provider.MediaStore.EXTRA_MEDIA_FOCUS) ?: "no focus"}) from ${controller.packageName}: $request")
            if (request == VoiceRequest.Resume) return onPlaybackResumption(session, controller, true).await()
            val pick = try {
                tree.voice(request)
            } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
                throw e
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                AudioDiagnostics.log("voice", "couldn't search: ${e.javaClass.simpleName} ${e.message.orEmpty()}")
                session.sendError(controller, SessionError(SessionError.ERROR_IO, tr(R.string.voice_search_offline)))
                throw VoiceNotFound(request)
            }
            if (pick == null) {
                AudioDiagnostics.log("voice", "nothing found")
                val heard = query?.trim()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() } ?: request.toString()
                session.sendError(controller, SessionError(SessionError.ERROR_BAD_VALUE, tr(R.string.voice_nothing_found, heard)))
                throw VoiceNotFound(request)
            }
            AudioDiagnostics.log("voice", "playing \"${pick.title}\": ${pick.songs.size} songs")
            c.queue.autoplayStart.value = -1
            c.queue.autoplayContinuation = null
            if (pick.radio) {
                val song = pick.songs.first()
                c.queue.source.value = QueueSource(QueueKind.SINGLE, pick.title, seedId = song.id)
                player.shuffleModeEnabled = false
                return MediaSession.MediaItemsWithStartPosition(listOf(song.toMediaItem()), 0, C.TIME_UNSET)
            }
            c.queue.source.value = QueueSource(QueueKind.PLAYLIST, pick.title)
            // Asked to shuffle: shuffled here too, so it's a new order each time (as the car's "Shuffle play" is);
            // otherwise in order, whatever shuffle was left on.
            // A new queue asked shuffled: DJ mode ends with the old one (Shuffle in DJ mode would deal a DJ set, #374).
            if (request.shuffle && pick.songs.size > 1) c.dj.setOn(false)
            player.shuffleModeEnabled = request.shuffle && pick.songs.size > 1
            val list = if (player.shuffleModeEnabled) pick.songs.shuffled() else pick.songs
            return MediaSession.MediaItemsWithStartPosition(list.map { it.toMediaItem() }, pick.start.coerceIn(0, list.lastIndex), C.TIME_UNSET)
        }

        /** Lets the system media controls (and the car) resume the last queue after Prism was closed. */
        override fun onPlaybackResumption(
            mediaSession: MediaSession,
            controller: MediaSession.ControllerInfo,
            isForPlayback: Boolean,
        ): ListenableFuture<MediaSession.MediaItemsWithStartPosition> = scope.future {
            val r = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { resumptionOf(runCatching { queueFile.readText() }.getOrNull(), keptPlace()) }
            AudioDiagnostics.log("control", "resume asked by ${controller.packageName}: " + (r?.let { "${it.songs[it.index].id} at ${it.positionMs / 1000} s" } ?: "nothing saved"))
            if (r == null) {
                MediaSession.MediaItemsWithStartPosition(emptyList(), 0, C.TIME_UNSET)
            } else {
                c.queue.source.value = QueueSource(QueueKind.OTHER, r.title)
                MediaSession.MediaItemsWithStartPosition(r.songs.map { it.toMediaItem() }, r.index, r.positionMs)
            }
        }
    }
    // ------------------------------------------------------------ Stats for nerds

    private inner class StatsListener : AnalyticsListener {
        private fun update(block: NerdStats.() -> NerdStats) {
            c.nerdStats.value = c.nerdStats.value.block()
        }

        override fun onAudioInputFormatChanged(
            eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime,
            format: Format,
            decoderReuseEvaluation: DecoderReuseEvaluation?,
        ) = update {
            copy(
                codec = format.codecs ?: format.sampleMimeType ?: codec,
                sampleRate = format.sampleRate.takeIf { it > 0 } ?: sampleRate,
                bitDepth = when (format.pcmEncoding) {
                    C.ENCODING_PCM_8BIT -> 8
                    C.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT, C.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT_BIG_ENDIAN -> 16
                    C.ENCODING_PCM_24BIT, C.ENCODING_PCM_24BIT_BIG_ENDIAN -> 24
                    C.ENCODING_PCM_32BIT, C.ENCODING_PCM_32BIT_BIG_ENDIAN, C.ENCODING_PCM_FLOAT -> 32
                    else -> 0
                },
                channels = format.channelCount.takeIf { it > 0 } ?: channels,
                container = format.containerMimeType ?: container,
                streamBitrateKbps = streamBitrateKbps.takeIf { it > 0 }
                    ?: (format.averageBitrate.takeIf { it > 0 } ?: format.bitrate.takeIf { it > 0 })?.let { it / 1000 } ?: 0,
            )
        }

        // The length isn't always known when a downloaded song starts; fill its bitrate in once it is.
        override fun onTimelineChanged(eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime, reason: Int) {
            val id = player.currentMediaItem?.mediaId ?: return
            if (c.nerdStats.value.streamBitrateKbps <= 0) applyStreamStats(id)
        }

        override fun onAudioDecoderInitialized(
            eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime,
            decoderName: String,
            initializedTimestampMs: Long,
            initializationDurationMs: Long,
        ) = update { copy(decoder = decoderName) }

        override fun onAudioTrackInitialized(eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime, audioTrackConfig: AudioSink.AudioTrackConfig) =
            update {
                copy(
                    outputEncoding = AudioEffects.encodingName(audioTrackConfig.encoding),
                    outputSampleRate = audioTrackConfig.sampleRate,
                    offload = audioTrackConfig.offload,
                    spatializer = c.settings.current.let { s ->
                        if (s.spatial) "Prism Spatial Â· ${(s.spatialAmount * 100).roundToInt()}%" else "Off"
                    },
                )
            }

        override fun onBandwidthEstimate(
            eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime,
            totalLoadTimeMs: Int,
            totalBytesLoaded: Long,
            bitrateEstimate: Long,
        ) = update { copy(bandwidthKbps = bitrateEstimate / 1000) }

        override fun onVideoInputFormatChanged(
            eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime,
            format: Format,
            decoderReuseEvaluation: DecoderReuseEvaluation?,
        ) = update {
            copy(
                videoResolution = "${format.width}Ã—${format.height}" + if (format.frameRate > 0) " @ ${format.frameRate.toInt()}fps" else "",
                videoCodec = format.codecs ?: format.sampleMimeType ?: "",
            )
        }

        override fun onDroppedVideoFrames(eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime, droppedFrames: Int, elapsedMs: Long) =
            update { copy(droppedFrames = this.droppedFrames + droppedFrames) }

        override fun onAudioUnderrun(
            eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime,
            bufferSize: Int,
            bufferSizeMs: Long,
            elapsedSinceLastFeedMs: Long,
        ): Unit {
            // Background scans back off while the sound is running dry (MixTempo.ease).
            MixTempo.lastUnderrunAt = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
            update { copy(underruns = underruns + 1) }
            AudioDiagnostics.session.underrun()
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ Playback diagnostics

    /** What the audio does, for [AudioDiagnostics] (only written while it's on). */
    private inner class DiagnosticsListener : AnalyticsListener {
        private fun at(t: AnalyticsListener.EventTime) = "at ${formatMs(t.currentPlaybackPositionMs)}"
        private fun formatMs(ms: Long) = if (ms < 0) "?" else "%d:%02d.%d".format(ms / 60_000, ms / 1000 % 60, ms / 100 % 10)
        private fun track(cfg: AudioSink.AudioTrackConfig) =
            "${AudioEffects.encodingName(cfg.encoding)} ${cfg.sampleRate} Hz, channel mask ${cfg.channelConfig}, buffer ${cfg.bufferSize} bytes" +
                (if (cfg.offload) ", offload" else "") + (if (cfg.tunneling) ", tunneling" else "")

        override fun onAudioTrackInitialized(eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime, audioTrackConfig: AudioSink.AudioTrackConfig) =
            AudioDiagnostics.log("track", "made: ${track(audioTrackConfig)} ${at(eventTime)}; outputs ${AudioDiagnostics.devices(this@PlaybackService)}")

        override fun onAudioTrackReleased(eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime, audioTrackConfig: AudioSink.AudioTrackConfig) =
            AudioDiagnostics.log("track", "released: ${track(audioTrackConfig)} ${at(eventTime)}")

        override fun onAudioUnderrun(eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime, bufferSize: Int, bufferSizeMs: Long, elapsedSinceLastFeedMs: Long) =
            AudioDiagnostics.log("underrun", "the output ran dry ${at(eventTime)}: buffer ${bufferSizeMs} ms, last fed ${elapsedSinceLastFeedMs} ms before")

        override fun onAudioSinkError(eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime, audioSinkError: Exception) =
            AudioDiagnostics.log("sink-error", "${audioSinkError.javaClass.simpleName}: ${audioSinkError.message} ${at(eventTime)}")

        override fun onAudioCodecError(eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime, audioCodecError: Exception) =
            AudioDiagnostics.log("codec-error", "${audioCodecError.javaClass.simpleName}: ${audioCodecError.message} ${at(eventTime)}")

        override fun onAudioPositionAdvancing(eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime, playoutStartSystemTimeMs: Long) =
            AudioDiagnostics.log("playing", "audio started coming out ${at(eventTime)}")

        override fun onAudioInputFormatChanged(eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime, format: Format, decoderReuseEvaluation: DecoderReuseEvaluation?) =
            AudioDiagnostics.log("format", "${format.codecs ?: format.sampleMimeType} ${format.sampleRate} Hz ${format.channelCount} ch, pcm ${format.pcmEncoding}")

        override fun onAudioDecoderInitialized(eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime, decoderName: String, initializedTimestampMs: Long, initializationDurationMs: Long) =
            AudioDiagnostics.log("decoder", "$decoderName (${initializationDurationMs} ms to start)")

        override fun onPlaybackStateChanged(eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime, state: Int) = AudioDiagnostics.log(
            "state",
            when (state) { Player.STATE_BUFFERING -> "buffering"; Player.STATE_READY -> "ready"; Player.STATE_ENDED -> "ended"; else -> "idle" } +
                " ${at(eventTime)}, ${(player.bufferedPosition - player.currentPosition).coerceAtLeast(0) / 1000} s loaded ahead",
        )

        override fun onIsPlayingChanged(eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime, isPlaying: Boolean) =
            AudioDiagnostics.log("state", (if (isPlaying) "playing" else "not playing (${player.playbackSuppressionReason.let { if (it == 0) "paused or waiting" else "suppressed: $it" }})") + " ${at(eventTime)}")

        // Why play/pause changed: a refused audio focus (Android 15+ refuses it to apps in the background without a
        // foreground service) shows up here as "audio focus loss" right after a play from another app (#187).
        override fun onPlayWhenReadyChanged(eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime, playWhenReady: Boolean, reason: Int) =
            AudioDiagnostics.log(
                "state",
                (if (playWhenReady) "play" else "pause") + " (" + when (reason) {
                    Player.PLAY_WHEN_READY_CHANGE_REASON_USER_REQUEST -> "asked"
                    Player.PLAY_WHEN_READY_CHANGE_REASON_AUDIO_FOCUS_LOSS -> "audio focus loss"
                    Player.PLAY_WHEN_READY_CHANGE_REASON_AUDIO_BECOMING_NOISY -> "headphones unplugged"
                    Player.PLAY_WHEN_READY_CHANGE_REASON_REMOTE -> "remote"
                    Player.PLAY_WHEN_READY_CHANGE_REASON_END_OF_MEDIA_ITEM -> "end of song"
                    Player.PLAY_WHEN_READY_CHANGE_REASON_SUPPRESSED_TOO_LONG -> "suppressed too long"
                    else -> "reason $reason"
                } + ") ${at(eventTime)}",
            )

        override fun onMediaItemTransition(eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime, mediaItem: MediaItem?, reason: Int) =
            AudioDiagnostics.log(
                "song",
                if (mediaItem == null) "none (the queue is empty), reason $reason"
                else "${mediaItem.mediaMetadata.title} â€“ ${mediaItem.mediaMetadata.artist} (${mediaItem.mediaId}), reason $reason",
            )

        override fun onPlayerError(eventTime: AnalyticsListener.EventTime, error: PlaybackException) =
            AudioDiagnostics.log("error", "${error.errorCodeName}: ${error.message} ${at(eventTime)}")
    }
}

/** Normalization's level: songs are brought to this loudness (integrated LUFS, as Spotify and YouTube use). */
/** How YouTube says a song can't be played where the listener is (#93). */
private val UNAVAILABLE = listOf("not available", "unavailable", "isn't available", "in your country")

/**
 * Whether [error] (or what caused it) is YouTube saying the song isn't available, in the listener's country mostly
 * (#93). Not age-restricted or Premium-only songs: those need an account, not another recording.
 */
internal fun unavailableHere(error: Throwable): Boolean =
    generateSequence(error) { it.cause }.any { e ->
        if (e is org.schabi.newpipe.extractor.exceptions.AgeRestrictedContentException ||
            e is org.schabi.newpipe.extractor.exceptions.YoutubeMusicPremiumContentException) return@any false
        e is org.schabi.newpipe.extractor.exceptions.ContentNotAvailableException ||
            e.message?.let { m -> UNAVAILABLE.any { m.contains(it, ignoreCase = true) } } == true
    }

/** Wear OS watches' companion apps (also in the manifest's `<queries>`): their media controls lay buttons out by slot. */
private val WATCH_COMPANIONS = listOf("com.google.android.apps.wear.companion", "com.google.android.wearable.app")

/** How a stream refusal reads when only an account (age, Premium) or another network would help, not another recording. */
private val NEEDS_MORE_THAN_A_VIDEO = listOf("age-restricted", "Premium", "limiting playback", "sign in to confirm")

/**
 * Whether a song that failed may play from its music video's sound instead (#93, Reddit R2): YouTube says it isn't
 * available here ([unavailableHere]), its stream couldn't be got, or its server kept turning it away ([refused], after
 * the retries). Never for age-restricted or Premium-only songs or a blocked network: the video won't help there.
 */
internal fun wontPlayHere(error: Throwable, refused: Boolean = false): Boolean {
    val chain = generateSequence(error) { it.cause }.toList()
    if (chain.any { e ->
            e is org.schabi.newpipe.extractor.exceptions.AgeRestrictedContentException ||
                e is org.schabi.newpipe.extractor.exceptions.YoutubeMusicPremiumContentException ||
                e.message?.let { m -> NEEDS_MORE_THAN_A_VIDEO.any { m.contains(it, ignoreCase = true) } } == true
        }) return false
    return unavailableHere(error) || refused || chain.any { it is com.prism.music.data.stream.StreamException }
}

/**
 * Whether a failure the player reports as the current song's is really about the next one: [failedSong]'s stream, the id
 * the failed lookup was for, read ahead for as the current one ends. [current] and [next]: each song's id and its video's.
 */
internal fun readAheadFailed(failedSong: String?, current: Set<String>, next: Set<String>): Boolean =
    failedSong != null && failedSong !in current && failedSong in next

/** How long a song that wouldn't play is shown, with its reason, before the next one starts. */
private const val SKIP_AFTER_FAILURE_MS = 1_500L
/** How long Autoplay's songs are waited for after a last song that wouldn't play. */
private const val AUTOPLAY_WAIT_MS = 15_000L

/**
 * Whether [error] is the audio decoder failing or going away rather than the song's bytes: Android releases a background
 * app's codecs when another one needs them (1.7.9's night check). Unreadable bytes are a [androidx.media3.common.ParserException],
 * handled on their own; a format this phone can't decode has its own error codes and isn't retried.
 */
internal fun stuckPlaying(error: Throwable): Boolean = generateSequence(error) { it.cause }.any {
    it is androidx.media3.common.util.StuckPlayerException && it.stuckType == androidx.media3.common.util.StuckPlayerException.STUCK_PLAYING_NO_PROGRESS
}

/** How many times a song whose position stopped moving is tried again ([stuckPlaying]) before it counts as failed. */
private const val STUCK_TRIES = 2

internal fun decoderLost(error: PlaybackException): Boolean =
    (error.errorCode == PlaybackException.ERROR_CODE_DECODING_FAILED || error.errorCode == PlaybackException.ERROR_CODE_DECODER_INIT_FAILED) &&
        generateSequence(error as Throwable) { it.cause }.none { it is androidx.media3.common.ParserException }

private const val TARGET_LUFS = -14f
/** The extra Media3 puts on the media button it sends when its notification is swiped away. */
private const val NOTIFICATION_DISMISSED = "androidx.media3.session.NOTIFICATION_DISMISSED_EVENT_KEY"
/** "Fade on pause and play" (A5): 8 steps of 30 ms. */
private const val PAUSE_FADE_STEPS = 8
private const val PAUSE_FADE_STEP_MS = 30L

/** How far a music video's length may be from its song's for its sound to play in the song's place (#93, Reddit R1). */
private const val SOUND_FROM_SLACK_SEC = 8

/** A song's volume (normalization) can still be set this far into it; after that it stays put for the song. */
private const val SETTLE_MS = 2_000L
/** A song already playing only has its volume changed for a better loudness figure this much apart (dB). */
private const val MIN_MIDSONG_CHANGE_DB = 1.5f
/** Queued songs are got ready once the playing one has played this long (not as it starts: that slowed its loading). */
private const val UNDER_WAY_MS = 5_000L
/**
 * How long after switching to a music video its measured offset may still move it to the matching moment (#203);
 * later the video stays where it is and only the lyrics follow the offset.
 */
private const val LATE_VIDEO_SEEK_MS = 3_000L

/** How far what plays may run from the song's own length before diagnostics note it ([PlaybackService.checkLength]). */
private const val LENGTH_MISMATCH_SEC = 8

/** Songs heard this recently aren't added by radio or autoplay again (1.7.9, Reddit: "the same songs over and over"). */
private const val RADIO_REST_MS = 4 * 3_600_000L
/** How long after a spoken request found its songs they may still come in and start playing. */
private const val VOICE_START_MS = 10_000L
/** How many songs ahead DJ mode scans and orders (a quick scan each: three stretches of the song, a few at once). */
private const val DJ_WINDOW = 40
/** How long DJ mode waits for more scans before putting the queue in order again. */
private const val RESEQUENCE_GAP_MS = 1_500L
/** How many songs ahead (in play order) Smart Shuffle places its picks among. */
private const val SMART_LOOK_AHEAD = 12
/** How many queued songs ahead are got ready (their loudness and edges). */
private const val QUEUE_AHEAD = 3
/** How far ahead the player loads: a whole song (and the start of the next, near its end). */
private const val WHOLE_SONG_MS = 300_000

/** About 10 seconds of a song's stream (Opus ~160 kbps, AAC up to 256 kbps), saved ahead by [AppContainer.warmUp]. */
private const val PREBUFFER_BYTES = 320L * 1024

class PrismApp : Application(), coil3.SingletonImageLoader.Factory {
    lateinit var container: AppContainer
        private set

    /**
     * Every image in Prism goes through here: covers saved on the phone are shown from their files (offline too, #54),
     * and data saver asks for smaller pictures on mobile data (#40). The rest is Coil's usual memory and disk caching.
     */
    override fun newImageLoader(context: coil3.PlatformContext): coil3.ImageLoader =
        coil3.ImageLoader.Builder(context)
            .components {
                // First, so a cover kept on the phone is trimmed the same as one from the network (#92).
                add(com.prism.music.data.VideoCoverInterceptor { ::container.isInitialized && container.settings.current.squareVideoCovers })
                add(com.prism.music.data.OfflineArtInterceptor { if (::container.isInitialized) container.artwork else null })
                // #520: local albums' covers sharp on the player and album page (lists keep the small one).
                add(com.prism.music.data.local.LocalFullArtInterceptor { if (::container.isInitialized) container.localMusic else null })
                add(com.prism.music.data.DataSaverInterceptor { ::container.isInitialized && container.dataSaving() })
            }
            .diskCache {
                coil3.disk.DiskCache.Builder()
                    .directory(File(context.cacheDir, "image_cache").absolutePath.toPath())
                    .maxSizePercent(0.02)
                    // #535: a TV box has 8 GB in all; pictures keep to less there.
                    .apply { if (com.prism.music.ui.tv.TvDevice.isTv(context)) maximumMaxSizeBytes(com.prism.music.data.TvCacheRoom.IMAGES) }
                    .build()
            }
            .build()

    override fun attachBaseContext(base: android.content.Context) = super.attachBaseContext(AppLanguage.wrap(base))

    /** The language Prism started in: a change of it from outside Prism restarts Prism ([onConfigurationChanged]). */
    private var language = ""

    override fun onConfigurationChanged(newConfig: android.content.res.Configuration) {
        super.onConfigurationChanged(newConfig)
        // Menus' labels and other text read once would stay in the old language (seen after a change in the phone's
        // Settings â†’ Apps â†’ Prism â†’ Language): Prism starts again in the new one, as its own Language page does.
        if (AppLanguage.changed(this, language, newConfig)) AppLanguage.restart(this)
    }

    /**
     * Never called on a phone (Android just ends the process); Robolectric calls it after each test. The app's work stops
     * and its database closes then: the library's lists kept reading a database Robolectric had already let go of, and
     * the test JVM crashed in SQLite's native code (2026-11-07, two full runs in a row).
     */
    override fun onTerminate() {
        if (::container.isInitialized) {
            // Cancelled and waited for (briefly): a read already inside SQLite finishes before the database closes. Work
            // waiting for the main thread can't finish while this holds it, hence the short wait (2 s made the unit tests
            // four times as long).
            runCatching {
                kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking {
                    kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeoutOrNull(300) { container.scope.coroutineContext[kotlinx.coroutines.Job]?.cancelAndJoin() }
                }
            }
            container.scope.cancel()
            runCatching { container.db.close() }
        }
        super.onTerminate()
    }

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        // Before anything shows a word: Prism's text in the listener's language (Strings.kt).
        Strings.resources = resources
        // Models.kt's words (kind names, song counts) come from the same place on Android.
        com.prism.music.data.model.ModelText.install(com.prism.music.data.model.AndroidModelText)
        language = AppLanguage.shown(this)
        // A crash's details kept on the phone (Settings â†’ About â†’ Diagnostics), from the very start.
        CrashLog.install(this)
        // A restore from a backup goes in before anything opens the files it replaces.
        com.prism.music.data.Backup.applyPending(this)
        // Before any icon is built: they're drawn in the style picked in Settings â†’ Appearance â†’ Icons.
        com.prism.music.ui.theme.IconStyle.load(this)
        com.prism.music.ui.theme.AppFonts.init(this)
        container = AppContainer(this)
        // How much of the processor heavy work may take: see BackgroundBudget.
        com.prism.music.playback.BackgroundBudget.where = {
            when {
                container.visible.value -> com.prism.music.playback.BackgroundBudget.Where.ON_SCREEN
                container.player.isPlaying.value || container.player.buffering.value -> com.prism.music.playback.BackgroundBudget.Where.PLAYING
                else -> com.prism.music.playback.BackgroundBudget.Where.AWAY
            }
        }
        com.prism.music.playback.BackgroundBudget.slowPhone = { com.prism.music.playback.MixTempo.slowPhone() }
        // Android's own backup restored onto another install: its sign-ins aren't this phone's (RestoredInstall).
        runCatching {
            com.prism.music.data.RestoredInstall.check(this) {
                // Signed out as Settings does: the account's library and Home kept for offline, and the sign-in page's
                // cookies (Android's backup leaves those out now, but an older backup may have brought them).
                container.settings.logout()
                runCatching { container.library.forgetAccount() }
                runCatching { android.webkit.CookieManager.getInstance().removeAllCookies(null) }
                runCatching { container.lastFm.signOut() }
                runCatching { container.discord.signOut() }
            }
        }
        // Whether any of Prism's screens is showing: what Android 12+ allows depends on it (DownloadRepository).
        registerActivityLifecycleCallbacks(object : ActivityLifecycleCallbacks {
            private var started = 0
            override fun onActivityStarted(activity: android.app.Activity) { started++; container.visible.value = true }
            override fun onActivityStopped(activity: android.app.Activity) { started = (started - 1).coerceAtLeast(0); container.visible.value = started > 0 }
            override fun onActivityCreated(activity: android.app.Activity, state: android.os.Bundle?) {}
            override fun onActivityResumed(activity: android.app.Activity) {}
            override fun onActivityPaused(activity: android.app.Activity) {}
            override fun onActivitySaveInstanceState(activity: android.app.Activity, state: android.os.Bundle) {}
            override fun onActivityDestroyed(activity: android.app.Activity) {}
        })
        runCatching { com.prism.music.data.Notifications.createChannels(this) }
        // Long-press the app icon: Search, Recognize a song, Liked songs, Downloads (Reddit 1.7).
        // Off the main thread: it talks to the launcher's service and loads their icons, and start-up shouldn't wait.
        Thread({ runCatching { com.prism.music.AppShortcuts.publish(this) } }, "PrismShortcuts").start()
        // For you (1.7): Home gets its sections once, and Release Radar looks round every few days.
        container.settings.addNewHomeSections()
        // The scheduled jobs are set up off the main thread: WorkManager opens its own database the first time, and on a
        // slow phone start-up shouldn't wait for it (GitHub #103: a budget phone's Prism kept restarting).
        container.scope.launch(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) {
            runCatching { com.prism.music.data.foryou.ReleaseRadar.schedule(this@PrismApp) }
            // Rewind: the 1 December notification, and last year kept once it's over (after start-up has settled).
            runCatching { com.prism.music.data.Rewinds.schedule(this@PrismApp) }
            // The monthly recap's notice (1.8.5), posted only if its switch is on when the 1st comes.
            runCatching { com.prism.music.data.Recaps.schedule(this@PrismApp) }
            // DJ mode: the songs played most and liked get their key read once a day, on Wi-Fi while charging.
            runCatching { com.prism.music.playback.DjPrescanWorker.schedule(this@PrismApp) }
            // Bring your history: the daily look at whether a fresh Spotify or Takeout download is due (only once one
            // was imported; a restored backup brings the dates with it).
            runCatching { com.prism.music.data.imports.ImportReminders.schedule(this@PrismApp) }
        }
        // YouTube Music (live), 1.8.1: its job only while signed in and turned on; signing out stops it (read from its
        // preferences, so nothing is built for the many who never turn it on).
        container.scope.launch(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) {
            var was: Boolean? = null
            container.settings.flow.map { it.isLoggedIn }.distinctUntilChanged().collect { signedIn ->
                runCatching {
                    com.prism.music.data.imports.YouTubeLive.signInChanged(
                        this@PrismApp, signedIn, was, com.prism.music.data.imports.YouTubeLiveWorker.Jobs(this@PrismApp),
                    )
                }
                was = signedIn
            }
        }
        container.scope.launch(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) {
            kotlinx.coroutines.delay(45_000)
            container.quietNetwork()
            runCatching { container.rewind.freezeLastYear() }
        }
        // Scrobbles made offline go to Last.fm once the phone is back online (#133).
        container.scope.launch(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) {
            if (!container.lastFm.available) return@launch
            container.network.online.collect { online -> if (online && container.lastFm.user.value != null) runCatching { container.lastFm.flush() } }
        }
        // A restored backup's settings (since 1.7.8 kept as values beside the store) go in at once, before Home shows.
        container.scope.launch(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) { runCatching { container.backup.applyRestoredSettings() } }
        // Downloads a restore left waiting carry on once the first screen is up (the download manager's calls go on the
        // main thread).
        container.scope.launch(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.Main) {
            kotlinx.coroutines.delay(1_500)
            runCatching { container.backup.resumeDownloads() }
        }
        // Diagnostics also watch the screen's thread (1.7.4, #100): work that holds it up long enough to drop frames is
        // logged with where it was. The test copy (".dev") also says so in logcat (PrismStall).
        com.prism.music.playback.AudioDiagnostics.init(this)
        // How the last run ended, when it wasn't a Java crash (killed for memory, ANR, native crash: #103), off the
        // main thread (an ANR's trace can be large).
        container.scope.launch(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) { runCatching { CrashLog.recordExits(this@PrismApp) } }
        com.prism.music.playback.MainThreadWatch.toLogcat = BuildConfig.APPLICATION_ID.endsWith(".dev")
        container.scope.launch {
            container.settings.flow.map { it.audioDiagnostics }.distinctUntilChanged().collectLatest { on ->
                kotlinx.coroutines.withContext(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.Main) { com.prism.music.playback.MainThreadWatch.setEnabled(on) }
                // And what Prism's memory is made of, every half minute (#103/#134: the phone closed Prism with gigabytes
                // in use, and the kind that grew says where to look).
                if (on) kotlinx.coroutines.withContext(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) {
                    while (true) {
                        runCatching { com.prism.music.playback.AudioDiagnostics.log("memory", MemoryLine.now()) }
                        kotlinx.coroutines.delay(30_000)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        // A lyrics save into a music file that Prism closing cut short is put right (#16).
        if (java.io.File(filesDir, "lyrics-tags").list()?.isNotEmpty() == true) {
            container.scope.launch(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) { runCatching { container.lyricsToFiles.recover() } }
        }
        // Debug builds: song ids pushed to â€¦/files/scan_list.txt are scanned (tempo, loudness, key) and logged under
        // PrismMix, for tuning the analysis on real songs (with mix_dump there too, their decoded audio is kept).
        if (BuildConfig.DEBUG) getExternalFilesDir(null)?.let { java.io.File(it, "scan_list.txt") }?.takeIf { it.isFile }?.let { list ->
            container.scope.launch(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) {
                for (id in list.readLines().map { it.trim() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }) {
                    runCatching { container.mixTempo.measure(id, network = true, edges = true, key = true) }
                }
                android.util.Log.i("PrismMix", "scan list done")
                list.delete()
            }
        }
        // Debug builds: renderers by description address, one per line in â€¦/files/connect_renderer.txt, for casting to a
        // pretend one on a PC through adb (SSDP doesn't cross it); see SimulatedRendererTest.castFromThePhone. A
        // "googlecast://host:port" line adds a Google Cast device the same way (GoogleCastTest.castFromThePhone).
        if (BuildConfig.DEBUG) getExternalFilesDir(null)?.let { java.io.File(it, "connect_renderer.txt") }?.takeIf { it.isFile }?.let { f ->
            f.readLines().map { it.trim() }.filter { it.startsWith("http://") || it.startsWith("googlecast://") }.forEach { container.cast.addByLocation(it) }
        }
    }
}

val Context.container: AppContainer get() = (applicationContext as PrismApp).container

/** One line of what this process's memory is made of, for diagnostics (in MB, as Android's own meminfo counts it). */
internal object MemoryLine {
    fun now(): String {
        val mi = android.os.Debug.MemoryInfo().also { android.os.Debug.getMemoryInfo(it) }
        fun mb(stat: String) = (mi.getMemoryStat(stat)?.toLongOrNull() ?: 0L) / 1024
        val threads = java.io.File("/proc/self/task").list()?.size ?: Thread.activeCount()
        val rt = Runtime.getRuntime()
        return "total ${mb("summary.total-pss")} MB (Java ${mb("summary.java-heap")}, native ${mb("summary.native-heap")}, " +
            "graphics ${mb("summary.graphics")}, code ${mb("summary.code")}, other ${mb("summary.private-other")}, swapped ${mb("summary.total-swap")}); " +
            "Java heap ${(rt.totalMemory() - rt.freeMemory()) / 1_048_576} of ${rt.maxMemory() / 1_048_576} MB; $threads threads; " +
            "processor since the last line ${cpuShare()}"
    }

    private var cpuAt = 0L
    private var wallAt = 0L

    /** Prism's whole process, as a share of one core (#373: Android closes a background app for this figure). */
    @Synchronized private fun cpuShare(): String {
        val cpu = android.os.Process.getElapsedCpuTime()
        val wall = android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
        val share = if (wallAt > 0 && wall > wallAt) "${(cpu - cpuAt) * 100 / (wall - wallAt)}% of a core" else "not known yet"
        cpuAt = cpu; wallAt = wall
        return share
    }
}

/** Hand-rolled dependency graph; everything lives for the app process. */
@OptIn(UnstableApi::class)
class AppContainer(val app: Application) {
    val scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.Default)

    /** One of Prism's screens is showing (an activity is started); set by [PrismApp]. */
    val visible = MutableStateFlow(false)

    /**
     * Shows Android's notification prompt (set by the activity on screen). It isn't asked at start-up any more, with
     * nothing on screen to say why: the tour's "Stay in the loop" step asks, or else the first download does.
     */
    @Volatile var notificationPrompt: (() -> Unit)? = null

    /**
     * Whether Prism had been set up before this run (welcome screen done, or signed in): the tour greets a new listener
     * and someone updating differently. Set once, when the activity first starts.
     */
    @Volatile var returningUser: Boolean? = null

    /**
     * Asks for notifications once ever, when Prism may not post them yet and is on screen to ask. Never over a coach tour
     * (1.8.8: First steps' like downloads the song, and Android's prompt covered the next step); not marked asked then,
     * so a later download asks.
     */
    fun askNotifications() {
        if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 33 || com.prism.music.data.Notifications.allowed(app)) return
        if (com.prism.music.ui.tour.TourAnchors.running) return
        val prefs = app.getSharedPreferences("prompts", android.content.Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        if (prefs.getBoolean("notifications_asked", false)) return
        val prompt = notificationPrompt ?: return
        prefs.edit().putBoolean("notifications_asked", true).apply()
        prompt()
    }
    val settings = SettingsRepository(app, scope)
    /** Looks (1.7.3): saved, Prism's own and shared looks, Undo for look changes, the day / night schedule. */
    val looks = com.prism.music.data.looks.LookStore(app, settings, scope)
    /** Looks people shared on GitHub (themes/ in Prism's repository), fetched only when the listener opens the list. */
    val lookGallery by lazy { com.prism.music.data.looks.LookGallery(http, java.io.File(app.filesDir, "looks"), { settings.lookKinds }) }
    /** "Customize this" (1.7.3): with it on, the mini player, tab bar, player and Home's sections open their own settings. */
    val customizing = MutableStateFlow(false)

    val http: OkHttpClient = OkHttpClient.Builder()
        .connectTimeout(15, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .readTimeout(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .apply { TestHooks.network?.let(::addInterceptor) }
        .build()
    /**
     * googlevideo's links. 1.8.7 (#408): each fetch notes its connection's address family (never the address), the
     * resolver hears how each link fared, and links asked for over IPv4 only are fetched over IPv4 only too.
     */
    internal val streamHttp: OkHttpClient = run {
        val chunks = http.newBuilder().addNetworkInterceptor(com.prism.music.data.stream.NetFamily.Tag).build()
        val ipv4 = chunks.newBuilder().dns(com.prism.music.data.stream.Ipv4OnlyDns()).build()
        val watch = com.prism.music.data.stream.StreamWatch { url, code, family -> resolverMade?.fetched(url, code, family) }
        http.newBuilder()
            .addInterceptor(YouTubeStreamInterceptor(chunks, ipv4Client = ipv4, watch = watch))
            .addNetworkInterceptor(com.prism.music.data.stream.NetFamily.Tag)
            .build()
    }

    /** The resolver once it's made: a fetch before then (a Canvas clip) has nothing to tell it, and doesn't make it. */
    @Volatile private var resolverMade: StreamResolver? = null

    val innerTube = InnerTube(http, { settings.current }, settings::setVisitorData)
    val ytm = YouTubeMusic(
        innerTube,
        songsEverywhere = { settings.current.let { it.radioSongsOnly && it.songsEverywhere } },
        onPairs = { pairs ->
            pairs.forEach { (song, video) -> queue.counterparts[song.id] = video }
            plays.learnAll(pairs.map { (song, video) -> video to song }, com.prism.music.data.meta.PairSource.ALBUM_PAGE)
        },
    ).apply { moodsCache = com.prism.music.data.MoodsCache(app.filesDir) }
    val db = PrismDatabase.build(app)
    /** Versions of songs the listener picked to hear instead (1.7.9, "Play a different version"). */
    val versions = com.prism.music.data.SongVersions(app.getSharedPreferences("song_versions", android.content.Context.MODE_PRIVATE))
    /** 1.8.7 Â§3.2: what plays for each row, one answer for playback, downloads, covers, history and lyrics. */
    val plays = com.prism.music.data.meta.PlaysAs(com.prism.music.data.meta.PlaysAs.fileIn(app.filesDir), scope, pickOf = { versions.picks.value[it] })
    val streams by lazy { StreamResolver(app, http, settings, innerTube).apply { versionOf = versions::streamedOf; resolverMade = this } }
    val translator = com.prism.music.data.lyrics.LyricsTranslator(http, File(app.filesDir, "translations"))
    /** Artist pages' Wikipedia bios (1.8.2), kept with the artist pages. Asks nothing unless that switch is on. */
    val artistBios by lazy {
        val wikipedia = com.prism.music.data.WikipediaBios(http, "Prism/${BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME} (https://github.com/Yigder/Prism)")
        com.prism.music.data.ArtistBios(File(app.filesDir, "offline/bios"), wikipedia::find)
    }
    val network = com.prism.music.data.NetworkState(app)
    /** Covers kept on the phone for downloads and the library, so they show offline. */
    val artwork = com.prism.music.data.ArtworkStore(app, http) { quietNetwork() }
    /** The library's lists and opened playlists and albums, kept so they open offline. */
    val offline = com.prism.music.data.OfflineLibrary(app)
    /** The music the listener uploaded to YouTube Music (Library â†’ Uploads). */
    val uploads by lazy { com.prism.music.data.Uploads(this) }
    /** The last Home, shown at once at the next start while the new one loads (#126). */
    val homeCache = com.prism.music.data.HomeCache(app.filesDir)
    /** Albums and playlists played lately (Home's Recently played, #196). */
    val recentCollections by lazy { com.prism.music.data.RecentCollections(app) }
    /** Last.fm scrobbling, when the listener connects their account (#133). */
    val lastFm by lazy { com.prism.music.data.lastfm.LastFm(app, http, scope) }
    val lyrics = LyricsRepository(http, db.lyrics(), ytm) { settings.current }.apply {
        choices = app.getSharedPreferences("lyrics_choice", android.content.Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        searchNames = app.getSharedPreferences("lyrics_search_as", android.content.Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        wordShifts = app.getSharedPreferences("lyrics_word_shift", android.content.Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        online = { network.isOnline() }
        // #344: the same files "Save lyrics into your music files" writes to, read only once they're switched on.
        val files = com.prism.music.data.local.EmbeddedLyricsFiles(app) { s ->
            if (s.isLocal) localMusic.uriFor(s.id) else localLossless.match(s)?.uri
        }
        fileLyrics = { s -> if (settings.current.behavior.embeddedLyrics) files.lyricsOf(s) else null }
    }
    /** Search by a line of the lyrics, from the box itself (1.7.6). */
    val lyricSearch = com.prism.music.data.lyrics.LyricSearch(http, ytm, db.lyricsSearch(), db.songs(), lyrics::textLines)
    val meta = MetaRepository(http, db.meta())
    val canvas = com.prism.music.data.canvas.CanvasRepository(http).apply {
        hiddenPrefs = app.getSharedPreferences("canvas_hidden", android.content.Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        savedPrefs = app.getSharedPreferences("canvas_saved", android.content.Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        useIndex(File(app.filesDir, "canvas_index.json"))
        // 1.8.7.6, "Spotify's canvas first": asked only with its switch on and the Spotify connection the web-player
        // session (read from its file alone: nothing is built or unsealed for it), whatever Prism's own two Canvas
        // switches say; Performance mode pauses it (the settings used read it as off).
        prismCovers = { com.prism.music.data.canvas.prismCoversOn(settings.current) }
        spotify = com.prism.music.data.canvas.SpotifyCanvasSource(
            CoroutineScope(scope.coroutineContext + Dispatchers.IO),
            enabled = {
                com.prism.music.data.canvas.spotifyCanvasOn(settings.current, com.prism.music.data.spotify.SpotifyStore.sessionIn(spotifyAuthPrefs))
            },
            finder = { spotifyCanvasFinder },
            quietUntil = { this@AppContainer.spotify.web.quietUntil },
            file = File(app.filesDir, "spotify_canvas.json"),
            log = { com.prism.music.playback.AudioDiagnostics.log("spotify", it) },
        )
    }
    /** The Spotify connection's own file ([com.prism.music.data.spotify.SpotifyStore.AUTH_PREFS]), read without building it. */
    private val spotifyAuthPrefs by lazy { app.getSharedPreferences(com.prism.music.data.spotify.SpotifyStore.AUTH_PREFS, android.content.Context.MODE_PRIVATE) }
    /** Spotify's canvas through the session's reads; built on the first ask. */
    private val spotifyCanvasFinder by lazy { com.prism.music.data.spotify.SpotifyCanvas { op, vars -> spotify.web.read(op, vars) } }
    init {
        // Whether the session sign-in is on, for the rows and search lines that show only then (read off the main thread).
        scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) { com.prism.music.data.spotify.SpotifySessionNow.watch(spotifyAuthPrefs) }
    }
    val videoSync by lazy { com.prism.music.playback.VideoSync(app, streamHttp, streams, scope) }
    /** Songs' tempo and edges, for Mix, and their loudness, for normalization. */
    val mixTempo by lazy {
        // Phones with few cores (or little memory, or Performance mode on): scans make way while the listener scrolls (#343).
        val slow = com.prism.music.playback.MixTempo.isSlowPhone(Runtime.getRuntime().availableProcessors(), com.prism.music.data.DevicePower.mayBeSlow(app), false)
        com.prism.music.playback.MixTempo.slowPhone = { slow || settings.current.performanceMode }
        com.prism.music.playback.MixTempo(app, streamHttp, streams, ::audioOnPhone, { yieldToPlaying(null) }, { player.isPlaying.value }, waves, traits)
    }
    /** What's measured of songs (tempo, key, loudness, energy), in one table that smart playlists filter on (1.8.3). */
    val traits by lazy { com.prism.music.data.TraitsStore(db.traits(), scope) }
    /** Songs' loudness outlines, for the waveform progress bar (made by the song scan). */
    val waves by lazy { com.prism.music.data.SongWaves(app) }

    /**
     * Where a song's audio is on the phone, for scanning it without the network: a local file as it is; a download or
     * a song cached whole, copied out of its cache into [scratch]. Null when it isn't all here.
     */
    internal fun audioOnPhone(id: String, scratch: File): android.net.Uri? {
        if (id.startsWith(com.prism.music.data.model.Song.LOCAL_PREFIX)) return localMusic.uriFor(id)
        for ((cache, key) in listOf(downloadCache to id, playerCache to songCacheKey(id))) {
            if (!cache.fullyCached(key, 0, C.LENGTH_UNSET.toLong())) continue
            val ok = runCatching {
                val source = CacheDataSource(cache, null)
                source.open(DataSpec.Builder().setUri("prism://audio/$id").setKey(key).build())
                try {
                    scratch.outputStream().use { out ->
                        val buf = ByteArray(64 * 1024)
                        while (true) {
                            val n = source.read(buf, 0, buf.size)
                            if (n == C.RESULT_END_OF_INPUT) break
                            out.write(buf, 0, n)
                        }
                    }
                } finally { source.close() }
            }.isSuccess
            if (ok) return android.net.Uri.fromFile(scratch)
        }
        return null
    }
    /** Per-song lyric timing nudges, in ms (positive = lyrics earlier). Keyed by song id, plus ":video" in video mode. */
    /** YouTube's loudness figure per song (dB from its reference level), kept for offline normalization. */
    val loudness: android.content.SharedPreferences = app.getSharedPreferences("loudness", android.content.Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
    val lyricOffsets: android.content.SharedPreferences = app.getSharedPreferences("lyric_offsets", android.content.Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    init {
        // Misses are cheap to re-check and may have been wrong (e.g. a provider was down).
        scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) { runCatching { db.lyrics().clearMisses() } }
    }
    val queue = QueueState()
    val nerdStats = MutableStateFlow(NerdStats())
    /** #519: Android refuses the effects the equalizer asks for (the playback service sets it; the Equalizer screen says so). */
    val eqRefused = MutableStateFlow(false)
    /** video id -> (source label, resolved stream) for the stats overlay */
    val streamDetails = ConcurrentHashMap<String, Pair<String, ResolvedStream?>>()
    /** song id -> the file its saved copy (Downloaded / Cached) came as, while that copy plays (1.8.7, #373). */
    private val copyFormats = ConcurrentHashMap<String, CachedFormat>()

    /**
     * Stats for nerds' line on where [songId]'s quality comes from: the saved copy that plays and its format, or Premium's
     * best held back by the mobile data quality. Null when there's nothing to say.
     */
    fun streamNote(songId: String): String? {
        val (source, s) = streamDetails[songId] ?: return null
        return com.prism.music.ui.player.streamQualityNote(source, s?.heldBack, copyFormats[songId])
    }
    @Volatile var onStreamResolved: ((String) -> Unit)? = null

    val databaseProvider by lazy { StandaloneDatabaseProvider(app) }
    /** Bytes Smart Downloads' picks take up (kept current by [DownloadRepository]); the song cache has the rest of its space. */
    @Volatile var smartPickBytes = 0L
    /** The song cache keeps within Smart Downloads' space, less what its picks use. */
    val cacheEvictor by lazy {
        com.prism.music.download.SmartCacheEvictor(
            maxBytes = { (settings.current.offlineGb * 1_073_741_824.0).toLong() - smartPickBytes },
            favoured = { id -> runCatching { id in library.likedIds.value }.getOrDefault(false) },
            totalBytes = { (settings.current.offlineGb * 1_073_741_824.0).toLong() },
            // #535: on a TV box (8 GB in all) the song cache keeps to what the box can spare.
            roomCap = { used -> if (isTv) com.prism.music.data.TvCacheRoom.songCache(freeSpace(), used) else Long.MAX_VALUE },
        )
    }
    /** #535: a TV (Android TV / Google TV): the caches keep to [com.prism.music.data.TvCacheRoom]'s smaller rooms. */
    val isTv: Boolean by lazy { com.prism.music.ui.tv.TvDevice.isTv(app) }
    private val freeSpace by lazy { com.prism.music.data.TvCacheRoom.FreeSpace { app.cacheDir.usableSpace } }
    /** Recently played animated covers and loops together. */
    private val canvasRecentBytes: Long get() = if (isTv) com.prism.music.data.TvCacheRoom.CANVAS else 400L * 1024 * 1024
    val playerCache: SimpleCache by lazy {
        SimpleCache(File(app.cacheDir, "song_cache"), cacheEvictor, databaseProvider)
    }
    /** Shrinks the song cache to fit, after Smart Downloads' space or its picks changed. */
    fun trimSongCache() { scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) { runCatching { cacheEvictor.trim(playerCache) } } }
    val downloadCache: SimpleCache by lazy {
        SimpleCache(File(app.filesDir, "downloads"), NoOpCacheEvictor(), databaseProvider)
    }

    val artistPrefs = com.prism.music.data.ArtistPrefs(app)
    /** The color picker's recent and starred colors. */
    val savedColors = com.prism.music.data.prefs.SavedColors(app)
    /** Where each podcast episode was left off. */
    val episodes = com.prism.music.data.EpisodeProgress(app)
    /** Podcasts added by their RSS feeds: on the phone, no YouTube (Podcasts â†’ Add a podcast by RSS). */
    val rssPodcasts by lazy {
        com.prism.music.data.podcast.RssPodcasts(
            File(app.filesDir, com.prism.music.data.podcast.RssPodcasts.DIR),
            com.prism.music.data.podcast.RssGuard.feedClient(TestHooks.network),
        )
    }
    val library = LibraryRepository(this)
    /** Liked songs mirrored into a real YouTube Music playlist, when the listener turns that on. */
    val likedPlaylist = com.prism.music.data.LikedPlaylist(this)
    val localLossless = com.prism.music.data.local.LocalLossless(app)
    /** Library â†’ Local music: the phone's own music files. */
    val localMusic = com.prism.music.data.local.LocalMusic(app, scope) { settings.current.behavior.localSkipFolders }.also { music ->
        music.start()
        // #207, #249: folders ticked or unticked (or brought back by a restored backup).
        scope.launch { settings.flow.map { it.behavior.localSkipFolders }.distinctUntilChanged().drop(1).collect { music.rescan() } }
    }
    val losslessSync by lazy { com.prism.music.download.LosslessSync(app, this) }
    // Started only after localLossless exists: a launch from an earlier init block can run on a
    // background thread before this property is assigned.
    init { losslessSync.start() }
    val taste = com.prism.music.data.TasteRepository(this)
    /** Big data files downloaded on first use (the Japanese reading dictionary, Sing's model). */
    val models = com.prism.music.data.ModelStore(app, http)
    /** Playlists and saved albums kept on the phone (no account needed). */
    val prismLibrary = com.prism.music.data.PrismLibrary(java.io.File(app.filesDir, "library")).also { lib ->
        lib.onDeleted = { id -> scope.launch(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) { runCatching { transitions.forgetPlaylist(id) } } }
    }
    /**
     * The listener's own transitions between two songs of a playlist (1.8.8, Mix it yourself): read when the queue's
     * playlist is asked about, never at start. Made once, it clears out rows unused for a year.
     */
    val transitions by lazy {
        com.prism.music.data.TransitionStore(db.transitions(), scope).also { s ->
            scope.launch(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) { runCatching { s.prune() } }
        }
    }
    /** The listener's own tags on songs (1.8.3). */
    val tags by lazy { com.prism.music.data.TagStore(db, scope, java.io.File(app.filesDir, "library")) }
    /** Smart playlists' songs (1.8.3): asked for when one opens, never at start. */
    val smart by lazy {
        com.prism.music.data.SmartPlaylists(db, { downloads.completedSongs() }, { localMusic.tracks.value.map { it.song } }, { meta.cachedGenres(it) })
    }
    /**
     * Whole playlists for Library â†’ Songs' "From" (1.8.3, #382): Prism's own from the phone, YouTube's page after page
     * (the copy kept on the phone without a network). Only a whole read is kept (#473): a page that doesn't come is asked
     * again, and a short or cancelled read is read again next time.
     */
    val playlistSongs by lazy {
        com.prism.music.data.PlaylistSongs { id ->
            prismLibrary.list(id)?.let { com.prism.music.data.PlaylistRead(it.songs, complete = true) } ?: run {
                val page = try { ytm.playlist(id) } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) { throw e } catch (e: Exception) {
                    // Offline: the copy on the phone is shown, but read live again next time.
                    val kept = offline.page(id) ?: offline.page("VL$id") ?: throw e
                    return@PlaylistSongs com.prism.music.data.PlaylistRead(kept.songs.distinctBy { it.id }, complete = false)
                }
                com.prism.music.data.readPlaylist(page, ytm::playlistPage)
            }
        }
    }
    /** "Measure now" on a smart playlist: the songs its Sound rules can't see yet, scanned one by one (Wi-Fi for streams). */
    val smartMeasure by lazy {
        com.prism.music.data.SmartMeasure(scope, { id, network -> mixTempo.measure(id, network = network, key = true) }) {
            app.getSystemService(android.net.ConnectivityManager::class.java)?.isActiveNetworkMetered == false
        }
    }
    /** The listener's Prism Account: a name and picture kept on this phone ([com.prism.music.data.PrismAccount]). */
    val account by lazy { com.prism.music.data.PrismAccount(this) }

    /**
     * What Prism calls the listener: their Prism Account's name, else their YouTube Music account's, else "".
     * [first]: just the first name ("Sam's Phone").
     */
    fun displayName(first: Boolean = false): String {
        val name = account.profile.value?.name?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: settings.current.accountName
        return if (first) name.trim().substringBefore(' ') else name.trim()
    }
    /** Lyrics in Latin letters (Korean, Japanese, Chinese, Arabic, Hindiâ€¦). */
    val romanizer = com.prism.music.data.lyrics.Romanizer(
        http, db.romanized(), models, scope, app.getSharedPreferences("romanize_songs", android.content.Context.MODE_PRIVATE),
    )

    val titleSpellings by lazy {
        com.prism.music.data.TitleSpellings(db.romanized(), scope, { romanizer.japaneseReady }) { romanizer.spellTitle(it) }
    }

    /** One-off upkeep that runs once per install, in the background, after Prism has settled. */
    private val migrations: android.content.SharedPreferences = app.getSharedPreferences("migrations", android.content.Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
    init {
        // Genre detection picked the wrong artist when several share a name (and now finds second
        // genres), so saved genres are looked up again once. Retried on a later launch if offline.
        if (!migrations.getBoolean("genres_refreshed_1", false)) scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            kotlinx.coroutines.delay(15_000)
            val songs = runCatching {
                (db.songs().liked() + db.songs().recentFlow(300).first()).map { it.toSong() }.distinctBy { it.id }
            }.getOrNull() ?: return@launch
            if (meta.refreshAll(songs)) migrations.edit().putBoolean("genres_refreshed_1", true).apply()
        }
        // Songs' genres come from iTunes (each song's own, by its artist's songs there): the refiner asks for one artist
        // at a time in the background, and the artists of the listener's own music are queued once, most played first.
        meta.startRefining(scope)
        if (!migrations.getBoolean("genres_itunes_1", false)) scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            kotlinx.coroutines.delay(20_000)
            val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
            val played = runCatching { db.plays().topArtists(now - 365L * 86_400_000, now, 200).map { it.artistName } }.getOrDefault(emptyList())
            val mine = runCatching { (db.songs().liked() + db.songs().recentFlow(300).first()).map { it.toSong() } }.getOrDefault(emptyList())
                .filter { !it.isLocal && !it.isEpisode }.distinctBy { it.id }.groupBy { it.primaryArtist }
            (played + mine.keys).filter { it.isNotBlank() }.distinct().forEach { meta.refineLater(it, mine[it].orEmpty()) }
            migrations.edit().putBoolean("genres_itunes_1", true).apply()
        }
        // 1.8.3: what earlier versions measured (in the scans' prefs) goes into the song_traits table, for the songs it has
        // no row for. Again after a restore that brought one without the other; nothing to do otherwise (a count).
        scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            kotlinx.coroutines.delay(25_000)
            runCatching {
                val m = mixTempo
                val copied = traits.copyMissing(m.measuredIds(), m::traitsFromPrefs)
                if (copied > 0) android.util.Log.i("PrismTraits", "copied $copied songs' measurements into song_traits")
            }
        }
        // 1.8.3: a restored backup's tags, read in once (a file check otherwise).
        scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            if (java.io.File(app.filesDir, "library/" + com.prism.music.data.TagStore.PENDING).isFile) tags.readPending()
        }
        // 1.8.8: a restored backup's transitions (they go with the Library), read in once (a file check otherwise).
        scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val f = java.io.File(app.filesDir, "library/" + com.prism.music.data.TransitionStore.PENDING)
            if (f.isFile) transitions.readPending(f)
        }
        // GitHub #182: bringing the library over had starred every library song's artist; those come off once, unless the
        // account follows them. Retried on a later launch when the account's follows couldn't be asked for.
        scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            kotlinx.coroutines.delay(12_000)
            if (artistPrefs.starsRepaired) return@launch
            val signedIn = settings.current.isLoggedIn
            val followed = if (signedIn) runCatching { ytm.librarySubscriptions().map { it.id }.toSet() }.getOrNull() else null
            artistPrefs.repairBroughtStars(followed, signedIn)
        }
    }
    val downloads by lazy { DownloadRepository(app, this, scope) }

    /**
     * What's downloaded, for screens: empty until [downloads] has started, then kept in step by it. Screens read these

    /**

     * music videos downloaded as their song, and (1.8.7 Â§3.2) while videos play as their songs, every video whose song
     * [plays] knows ([com.prism.music.data.meta.PlaysAsRules.keptAs]).

    val downloadStandIns = kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow<Map<String, String>>(emptyMap())

    @Volatile private var songStartedAt = 0L
    init { scope.launch { player.currentSong.collect { songStartedAt = android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() } } }

    /**
     * Waits until the network is free for background saving (offline copies of playlists, covers, download retries):
     * not while the music is buffering, nor in a song's first 20 s, when its stream is loading. Since 1.6.8 that saving
     * ran at every launch and sync, and on slower connections the music stalled behind it.
     */
    suspend fun quietNetwork() {
        while (player.buffering.value ||
            (player.isPlaying.value && android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - songStartedAt < 20_000)
        ) kotlinx.coroutines.delay(2_000)
    }

            // pairings only while its pairing screen is open.
            settings.flow.map { it.handoffAvailable }.distinctUntilChanged().collect { on -> handoff.setAvailable(on || handoff.isTv) }
